- **CEC Bank:** Please OCR two CEC Bank PDFs that are image-only scans and extract (i) intangible-asset/software additions and property-and-equipment additions for 2025, (ii) any ATM/MFM/POS or branch-count table, (iii) any named IT supplier or outsourcing arrangement: https://www.cec.ro/sites/default/files/2026-05/Raportul%20Consiliului%20de%20Administratie_CEC%20Bank_%2031.12.2025.pdf and https://www.cec.ro/sites/default/files/2026-05/Situatii%20financiare%20IFRS%20la%2031%20dec.2025.pdf . Also, if an operator can reach consiliulconcurentei.ro (503 to us), retrieve the official ROBOR decision press release / decision text confirming CEC Bank's RON 332.98m fine.
- **Raiffeisen Bank Romania:** 1) Consiliul Concurentei reasoned decision in the ROBOR case (RON 442.49m against Raiffeisen Bank Romania, plenum 07/06/2026) once published - the site is JS-gated and needs a real browser session. 2) Consiliul Concurentei authorisation decision on the Raiffeisen / Garanti Bank / Motoractive IFN / Motoractive Multiservices concentration when issued (expected before the Q4-2026 closing), same access problem. 3) Garanti Bank S.A. audited IFRS financial statements and Pillar III report for 31/12/2025, if published on garantibbva.ro - needed for a current branch/ATM/POS/employee count on the integration target. 4) RBI group H1-2026 interim report, Romania segment, once released (expected imminently after 30/07/2026).
- **UniCredit Bank Romania:** 1) UniCredit Bank S.A. standalone H1-2026 IFRS interim report - the bank is a BVB bond issuer (UCB27, UCB29) so a filing should exist; it would give the audited 30/06/2026 branch and ATM counts and any ROBOR provision. Not located on unicredit.ro this run. 2) The Romanian Competition Council's reasoned ROBOR decision (consiliulconcurentei.ro returned 503) - needed to confirm UniCredit's RON 431.03m share from the primary source and to read the mandated remediation obligations verbatim. 3) BNR's primary 'Indicatori plati' series at www.bnr.ro/Indicatori-plati-5291.aspx (302s to automated fetch) for the ATM/POS breakdown by deployer, to size UniCredit's share of the national estate properly.
- **DSK Bank (OTP):** Two items an operator could add value on: (1) OTP Bank Plc. Integrated Annual Report 2025 (https://www.otpgroup.info/static/sw/file/260417_Integrated_report_e_final_064.pdf) - a large PDF that timed out on local text extraction here; worth checking its supplier/procurement and digital-channel sections for any named self-service vendor at DSK. (2) The DSK ATM fleet OEM is still unnamed after an exhaustive open-web pass - the practical route now is a physical check: photograph the fascia/badging of a DSK deposit ATM (one 150-note site and one 40-note site) or ask the Printec Bulgaria field team, since no public document discloses it.
- **Postbank (Eurobank BG):** 1) FLEET OEM: a human/operator route is now the realistic way to name Postbank BG's ATM incumbent - e.g. a photograph of the fascia/screen of any Postbank ATM or Express Banking device (the OEM logo and model are usually visible on the bezel and boot screen), or a direct check with Printec Bulgaria's local team. 2) Bulgarian Commercial Register (registryagency.bg / portal.registryagency.bg) lookup to identify the software company Eurobank agreed in Feb-2026 to acquire for c. EUR 18m upfront plus earn-out - the registry search is behind a JS/SPA front end that is not reliably automatable.
- **UniCredit Bulbank:** None strictly required — every source used was retrievable without gating. One useful item if an operator has a moment: BORICA's monthly payment-statistics releases (specifically the December-2025 one showing ATM deposits up 85% to EUR 471m) are cited widely in Bulgarian press but were not findable on borica.bg; a direct link would let that figure be promoted from press to primary.
- **Banca Intesa Beograd:** 1) ASEE / Asseco South Eastern Europe management report for FY2025 and the full H1-2026 earnings-call transcript — needed for ATM-outsourcing capex, the Serbian ATM contract pipeline and any comment on the Banca Intesa renewal; the FY2025 PDF was not resolvable from asee.io this run. 2) The NBS draft amendments to the Law on Interbank Fees and Special Rules for Card-Based Payment Transactions (published for consultation ~16/06/2026) — only press coverage was found; the primary draft text and consultation deadline on nbs.rs were not located. 3) Official Gazette of the Republic of Serbia no. 43/2026, Decision on conditions and manner of establishing and verifying the identity of a natural person using means of electronic communication — full text needed to extract the technical eKYC requirements (liveness, recording, retention).
- **OTP banka Srbija:** 1) NBS quarterly banking-sector report ('Bankarski sektor u Srbiji') for Q4-2025 and Q1-2026 - the download list at https://www.nbs.rs/sr/finansijske-institucije/banke/izvestaji-i-analize/ is JS-rendered and no PDF URL could be resolved; an operator with a browser should grab the PDFs plus the accompanying 'aneks' .xls, which carries per-bank assets, branch counts and headcount and would let us verify OTP's 2025 market share and branch number against a regulator source. 2) OTP banka Srbija's 2025 standalone annual/financial report and 2025 sustainability report - links on https://www.otpbanka.rs/izvestavanje/ are JS-injected and unreachable to a fetcher. 3) The exact Official Gazette citation and entry-into-force date for the NBS 2026 Decision on identity verification by electronic communication means (remote/video onboarding), to fix the six-month and one-year compliance deadlines.
- **NLB Komercijalna banka:** 1) Field check, Belgrade: photograph the fascia and welcome screen of two or three NLB Komercijalna ATMs (for example Kneza Mihaila 38, Bulevar Mihajla Pupina 165v, Nikola Tesla Airport hall) to read the OEM badge and the ATM application branding - this is the fastest remaining route to naming the incumbent. 2) Ask NLB Group procurement in Ljubljana who won the ATM and Cash Deposit Machine (CDS) tenders finalised in 2025, and the framework term and call-off mechanics. 3) Check National Bank of Serbia supervisory filings for outsourcing / critical service provider notifications by NLB Komercijalna banka covering ATM operation and maintenance.
- **Raiffeisen banka Serbia:** 1) Complete the lead-generation form at https://offer.printecgroup.com/raiffeisen-bank-case-study to download the full Raiffeisen Bank Serbia case-study PDF - it is expected to contain device counts, go-live dates and the hardware vendor, which would settle the x-core incumbency question. 2) If an operator has a browser session, open https://www.nbs.rs/sr/ciljevi-i-funkcije/platni-sistem/statistika/ and export the per-bank ATM/POS breakdown if one is published there, since the page does not render to a headless fetch.
- **AikBank / AikGroup:** 1) BROWSER-RENDER THE AIKBANK LOCATOR: open https://www.aikbank.rs/lokacije/ in a real browser session and export the full branch and ATM list. This is the only route to an exact ATM count and, if the locator flags deposit-capable machines, to the multifunctional-versus-dispense-only split that the whole fleet-refresh business case depends on. 2) Do the same for the Gorenjska banka (Slovenia) and Hipotekarna banka (Montenegro) locators to confirm the 66 and 47 ATM figures independently and capture their deposit capability and OEM. 3) INTERNAL, not external: ask Printec Serbia to supply the contract evidence for the reported 75 NCR Atleos units at AikBank (models, delivery dates, cash-out versus MFM split) — it is not published anywhere retrievable and cannot otherwise be used as a reference. No paywalled or gated PDFs were encountered this run.
- **Hungary (OTP, K&H, Erste HU, MBH, Raiffeisen HU, CIB, Granit):** 1) K&H Bank press releases behind the kh.hu Akamai 403, needed for current K&H deposit-ATM volumes, cardless/NFC rollout status and automated-branch count: https://www.kh.hu/csoport/sajto/-/sajtohir/k-h-1-billio-forint-3-millio-befizetes-tarolnak-az-uj-atm-ek and https://www.kh.hu/csoport/sajto/-/sajtohir/forradalom-az-atm-eknel-rengeteg-tranzakciot-erint-az-uj-lepes 2) Annex 1 (the settlement lists) of MNB decisions H-KPL-I-B-1/2026, H-KPL-I-2/2026, H-KPL-I-3/2026 and H-KPL-I-4/2026 — request from the MNB under public-data access. These quantify the exact remaining ATM gap per bank and name the target settlements. 3) The MNB ERA register of Phase 2 ATM placement/takeover contracts reported by 16/03/2026 — would name the suppliers already contracted for the 31/12/2026 wave, which is the single highest-value unknown in this market. 4) Alkotmanybirosag case file/number for the OTP + Erste + Raiffeisen constitutional complaint of 10/11/2025 against Act XVIII of 2025, to track whether a ruling is scheduled.
- **Slovenia (NLB, OTP banka d.d., Intesa SI, Bankart, Addiko SI):** 1) Bankart d.o.o. annual report / financial statements from AJPES (https://www.ajpes.si) - needed to confirm or kill the 'authorisation-system replacement completing end-2026' claim and to re-source the 1,814 ATM / 55,000 POS estate figures; Bankart publishes nothing itself. 2) NLB Group Annual Report 2025 full PDF and the H1 2026 interim report when published on 06/08/2026 - check the Payments section for the ATM/CDS rollout vendor and unit counts. 3) The final Addiko takeover acceptance announcements (NLB investor news, takeover.at, addiko.com) published after 29/07/2026 - not yet indexed as of 31/07/2026.
- **Albania (Raiffeisen AL, BKT, Credins, OTP AL, Tirana Bank, Union Bank):** 1) Bank of Albania SUPERVISION ANNUAL REPORT 2025, presented 10/07/2026 (announcement page: https://www.bankofalbania.org/Shtypi/Njoftimet_per_shtyp/Raporti_Vjetor_i_Mbik_e_qyrjes-2025.html) — the PDF is not linked from any statically-fetchable index. Please retrieve it; it carries the per-bank supervisory picture and the payments-instrument chapter. 2) UNION BANK ALBANIA site (unionbank.al) — Cloudflare-blocked to all automated fetch. Needed: the Mastercard POSÍ programme page, the latest annual report, and any 2025/2026 ATM or recycler disclosure. A browser session or an operator download would close the single most important Printec incumbency account in this market. 3) app.gov.al e-procurement — a keyword crawl of "njoftimi i fituesit" and "njoftimi i kontratës së shpallur" for 2026 on the terms POS, POI, bankomat, ATM, terminal pagese. Public institutions with state capital had a 30/05/2026 POS deadline and their tenders are almost certainly on this portal; this needs a paginated crawl the fetch tool cannot do. 4) Bank of Albania Supervisory Council decisions 2026 — to obtain a primary source for the November-2026 instant-payments go-live and for the interchange regulation's consultation closing date.
